+void prepareDeviceMesh(const World& world, DeviceMesh& device) {
+    device.n_elems = world.elements_static.size();
+
+    if (device.n_elems > std::numeric_limits<uint32_t>::max()) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Mesh is too large for CUDA connectivity indices\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+    if (world.materials.size() > std::numeric_limits<uint8_t>::max()) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Too many material types for the CUDA mesh\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+
+    for (const ElementStatic& element : world.elements_static) {
+        if (element.num_connections > MAX_CONNECTIONS) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Element has more than %d connections\n",
+                         MAX_CONNECTIONS);
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        device.connections_per_element =
+            std::max(device.connections_per_element,
+                     static_cast<int>(element.num_connections));
+    }
+
+    std::vector<uint8_t> material_idx(device.n_elems);
+    std::vector<uint8_t> num_connections(device.n_elems);
+    const size_t connectivity_size =
+        device.n_elems * static_cast<size_t>(device.connections_per_element);
+    std::vector<uint32_t> connected_idx(connectivity_size);
+    std::vector<val_t> connected_flux(connectivity_size);
+
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < device.n_elems; ++i) {
+        const ElementStatic& element = world.elements_static[i];
+        if (element.material_idx >= world.materials.size()) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Element references an invalid material\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+
+        material_idx[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(element.material_idx);
+        num_connections[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(element.num_connections);
+        for (idx_t j = 0; j < element.num_connections; ++j) {
+            if (element.connected_idx[j] >= device.n_elems) {
+                std::fprintf(stderr, "Element references an invalid neighbor\n");
+                std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
             }
-            
-            // Update element state
-            ElementDynamic& elem_write = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i];
-            elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;
-            elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);
+            // Connection-major storage makes every load in a warp contiguous.
+            const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(j) * device.n_elems + i;
+            connected_idx[offset] =
+                static_cast<uint32_t>(element.connected_idx[j]);
+            connected_flux[offset] = element.connected_flux[j];
         }
-        
-        // Swap buffers
-        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
     }
+
+    deviceAllocate(device.material_idx, device.n_elems);
+    deviceAllocate(device.num_connections, device.n_elems);
+    deviceAllocate(device.connected_idx, connectivity_size);
+    deviceAllocate(device.connected_flux, connectivity_size);
+    deviceAllocate(device.materials, world.materials.size());
+    deviceAllocate(device.dynamic[0], device.n_elems);
+    deviceAllocate(device.dynamic[1], device.n_elems);
+
+    copyToDevice(device.material_idx, material_idx.data(), device.n_elems);
+    copyToDevice(device.num_connections, num_connections.data(), device.n_elems);
+    copyToDevice(device.connected_idx, connected_idx.data(), connectivity_size);
+    copyToDevice(device.connected_flux, connected_flux.data(), connectivity_size);
+    copyToDevice(device.materials, world.materials.data(), world.materials.size());
+    copyToDevice(device.dynamic[0], world.elements_dynamic.data(), device.n_elems);
+}
+
+template <int ConnectionsPerElement>
+__global__ void simulationIterationKernel(
+    const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+    const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+    const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+    const Material* __restrict__ materials,
+    const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ current,
+    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ next,
+    size_t n_elems) {
+    const size_t thread = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+
+    for (size_t i = thread; i < n_elems; i += stride) {
+        const ElementDynamic element = current[i];
+        const Material material = materials[material_idx[i]];
+        const int count = num_connections[i];
+        val_t total_flux = material.external_flow;
+
+        // Template specialization fully unrolls the small adjacency loop.  The
+        // conditional retains the exact operation count and order for boundary
+        // elements instead of padding them with arithmetic zeros.
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int j = 0; j < ConnectionsPerElement; ++j) {
+            if (j < count) {
+                const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(j) * n_elems + i;
+                const ElementDynamic neighbor = current[connected_idx[offset]];
+                total_flux +=
+                    (neighbor.current_energy - element.current_energy) *
+                    material.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[offset] * 0.25;
+            }
+        }
+
+        next[i].current_energy = element.current_energy + total_flux;
+        next[i].total_flux = element.total_flux + fabs(total_flux);
+    }
+}
+
+template <int ConnectionsPerElement>
+void launchSimulationIteration(DeviceMesh& device,
+                               const ElementDynamic* current,
+                               ElementDynamic* next,
+                               cudaStream_t stream) {
+    constexpr int threads_per_block = 256;
+    const size_t blocks_for_mesh =
+        (device.n_elems + threads_per_block - 1) / threads_per_block;
+    simulationIterationKernel<ConnectionsPerElement>
+        <<<static_cast<unsigned int>(blocks_for_mesh), threads_per_block, 0, stream>>>(
+            device.material_idx, device.num_connections, device.connected_idx,
+            device.connected_flux, device.materials, current, next, device.n_elems);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+}
+
+void launchSimulationIteration(DeviceMesh& device,
+                               const ElementDynamic* current,
+                               ElementDynamic* next,
+                               cudaStream_t stream) {
+    // Every supported degree gets a compile-time-unrolled CUDA specialization.
+    switch (device.connections_per_element) {
+        case 0: launchSimulationIteration<0>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 1: launchSimulationIteration<1>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 2: launchSimulationIteration<2>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 3: launchSimulationIteration<3>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 4: launchSimulationIteration<4>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 5: launchSimulationIteration<5>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 6: launchSimulationIteration<6>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 7: launchSimulationIteration<7>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 8: launchSimulationIteration<8>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        default:
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Unsupported CUDA mesh degree\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void emptySimulationKernel() {}
+
+// Capture all iterations once so the timed path needs only one CUDA graph
+// launch.  Consecutive graph nodes provide the required device-wide Jacobi
+// barrier without constraining the grid to cooperatively resident blocks.
+double runSimulation(DeviceMesh& device, const int n_iters) {
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t graph_instance = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeGlobal));
+
+    if (n_iters > 0) {
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+            const int read_buffer = iter & 1;
+            launchSimulationIteration(device, device.dynamic[read_buffer],
+                                      device.dynamic[read_buffer ^ 1], stream);
+        }
+    } else {
+        emptySimulationKernel<<<1, 1, 0, stream>>>();
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    }
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_instance, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    // Upload performs graph setup before the measured region without executing
+    // any node or changing the initial simulation state.
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(graph_instance, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_instance, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
+
+    float duration_ms = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&duration_ms, start, stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_instance));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));
+    return duration_ms;
+}
+
+void downloadSimulation(World& world, const DeviceMesh& device, int n_iters) {
+    const int final_buffer = n_iters > 0 && (n_iters & 1) ? 1 : 0;
+    if (device.n_elems != 0) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(),
+                              device.dynamic[final_buffer],
+                              device.n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
 }

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, int line) {

+inline void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                      const char* file, int line) {

+    }

+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    cudaCheck((expression), #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__)

+// The mesh is transposed to a structure-of-arrays representation before it is
+// uploaded.  A warp therefore fetches consecutive material IDs, connection
+// counts, neighbor IDs, and coefficients instead of 32 widely separated AoS
+// records.  Device indices can be 32-bit because the host program's element
+// count is an int, cutting the dominant connectivity traffic in half.
+struct DeviceMesh {
+    uint8_t* material_idx = nullptr;
+    uint8_t* num_connections = nullptr;
+    uint32_t* connected_idx = nullptr;
+    val_t* connected_flux = nullptr;
+    Material* materials = nullptr;
+    ElementDynamic* dynamic[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    size_t n_elems = 0;
+    int connections_per_element = 0;
+
+    DeviceMesh() = default;
+    DeviceMesh(const DeviceMesh&) = delete;
+    DeviceMesh& operator=(const DeviceMesh&) = delete;
+
+    ~DeviceMesh() {
+        cudaFree(dynamic[1]);
+        cudaFree(dynamic[0]);
+        cudaFree(materials);
+        cudaFree(connected_flux);
+        cudaFree(connected_idx);
+        cudaFree(num_connections);
+        cudaFree(material_idx);
+    }
+};

+template <int ConnectionsPerElement>
+__global__ void simulationIterationKernel(
+    const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+    const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+    const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+    const Material* __restrict__ materials,
+    const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ current,
+    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ next,
+    size_t n_elems) {
+    const size_t thread = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+
+    for (size_t i = thread; i < n_elems; i += stride) {
+        const ElementDynamic element = current[i];
+        const Material material = materials[material_idx[i]];
+        const int count = num_connections[i];
+        val_t total_flux = material.external_flow;
+
+        // Template specialization fully unrolls the small adjacency loop.  The
+        // conditional retains the exact operation count and order for boundary
+        // elements instead of padding them with arithmetic zeros.
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int j = 0; j < ConnectionsPerElement; ++j) {
+            if (j < count) {
+                const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(j) * n_elems + i;
+                const ElementDynamic neighbor = current[connected_idx[offset]];
+                total_flux +=
+                    (neighbor.current_energy - element.current_energy) *
+                    material.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[offset] * 0.25;
+            }
+        }
+
+        next[i].current_energy = element.current_energy + total_flux;
+        next[i].total_flux = element.total_flux + fabs(total_flux);
+    }
+}

+template <int ConnectionsPerElement>
+void launchSimulationIteration(DeviceMesh& device,
+                               const ElementDynamic* current,
+                               ElementDynamic* next,
+                               cudaStream_t stream) {
+    constexpr int threads_per_block = 256;
+    const size_t blocks_for_mesh =
+        (device.n_elems + threads_per_block - 1) / threads_per_block;
+    simulationIterationKernel<ConnectionsPerElement>

+            device.material_idx, device.num_connections, device.connected_idx,
+            device.connected_flux, device.materials, current, next, device.n_elems);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());

+void launchSimulationIteration(DeviceMesh& device,

+        case 4: launchSimulationIteration<4>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 5: launchSimulationIteration<5>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 6: launchSimulationIteration<6>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 7: launchSimulationIteration<7>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 8: launchSimulationIteration<8>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        default:
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Unsupported CUDA mesh degree\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void emptySimulationKernel() {}
+
+// Capture all iterations once so the timed path needs only one CUDA graph
+// launch.  Consecutive graph nodes provide the required device-wide Jacobi
+// barrier without constraining the grid to cooperatively resident blocks.
+double runSimulation(DeviceMesh& device, const int n_iters) {
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t graph_instance = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeGlobal));
+
+    if (n_iters > 0) {
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+            const int read_buffer = iter & 1;
+            launchSimulationIteration(device, device.dynamic[read_buffer],
+                                      device.dynamic[read_buffer ^ 1], stream);
+        }
+    } else {
+        emptySimulationKernel<<<1, 1, 0, stream>>>();
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    }
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_instance, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    // Upload performs graph setup before the measured region without executing
+    // any node or changing the initial simulation state.
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(graph_instance, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_instance, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
+
+    float duration_ms = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&duration_ms, start, stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_instance));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));
+    return duration_ms;
+}
+
+void downloadSimulation(World& world, const DeviceMesh& device, int n_iters) {
+    const int final_buffer = n_iters > 0 && (n_iters & 1) ? 1 : 0;
+    if (device.n_elems != 0) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(),
+                              device.dynamic[final_buffer],
+                              device.n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
 }

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n",
+                 file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);

+
+inline void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                      const char* file, int line) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        cudaFailure(error, expression, file, line);
+    }
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    cudaCheck((expression), #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__)
+
+// The mesh is transposed to a structure-of-arrays representation before it is
+// uploaded.  A warp therefore fetches consecutive material IDs, connection
+// counts, neighbor IDs, and coefficients instead of 32 widely separated AoS
+// records.  Device indices can be 32-bit because the host program's element
+// count is an int, cutting the dominant connectivity traffic in half.
+struct DeviceMesh {
+    uint8_t* material_idx = nullptr;
+    uint8_t* num_connections = nullptr;
+    uint32_t* connected_idx = nullptr;
+    val_t* connected_flux = nullptr;
+    Material* materials = nullptr;
+    ElementDynamic* dynamic[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    size_t n_elems = 0;
+    int connections_per_element = 0;
+
+    DeviceMesh() = default;
+    DeviceMesh(const DeviceMesh&) = delete;
+    DeviceMesh& operator=(const DeviceMesh&) = delete;
+
+    ~DeviceMesh() {
+        cudaFree(dynamic[1]);
+        cudaFree(dynamic[0]);
+        cudaFree(materials);
+        cudaFree(connected_flux);
+        cudaFree(connected_idx);
+        cudaFree(num_connections);
+        cudaFree(material_idx);
+    }
+};
+
+template <typename T>
+void deviceAllocate(T*& pointer, size_t count) {
+    static_assert(std::is_trivially_copyable_v<T>);
+    if (count != 0) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&pointer),
+                              count * sizeof(T)));
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void copyToDevice(T* destination, const T* source, size_t count) {
+    if (count != 0) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(destination, source, count * sizeof(T),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    }
+}
+
+void prepareDeviceMesh(const World& world, DeviceMesh& device) {
+    device.n_elems = world.elements_static.size();
+
+    if (device.n_elems > std::numeric_limits<uint32_t>::max()) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Mesh is too large for CUDA connectivity indices\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+    if (world.materials.size() > std::numeric_limits<uint8_t>::max()) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Too many material types for the CUDA mesh\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+
+    for (const ElementStatic& element : world.elements_static) {
+        if (element.num_connections > MAX_CONNECTIONS) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Element has more than %d connections\n",
+                         MAX_CONNECTIONS);
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        device.connections_per_element =
+            std::max(device.connections_per_element,
+                     static_cast<int>(element.num_connections));
+    }
+
+    std::vector<uint8_t> material_idx(device.n_elems);
+    std::vector<uint8_t> num_connections(device.n_elems);
+    const size_t connectivity_size =
+        device.n_elems * static_cast<size_t>(device.connections_per_element);
+    std::vector<uint32_t> connected_idx(connectivity_size);
+    std::vector<val_t> connected_flux(connectivity_size);
+
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < device.n_elems; ++i) {
+        const ElementStatic& element = world.elements_static[i];
+        if (element.material_idx >= world.materials.size()) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Element references an invalid material\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+
+        material_idx[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(element.material_idx);
+        num_connections[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(element.num_connections);
+        for (idx_t j = 0; j < element.num_connections; ++j) {
+            if (element.connected_idx[j] >= device.n_elems) {
+                std::fprintf(stderr, "Element references an invalid neighbor\n");
+                std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
             }
-            
-            // Update element state
-            ElementDynamic& elem_write = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i];
-            elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;
-            elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);
+            // Connection-major storage makes every load in a warp contiguous.
+            const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(j) * device.n_elems + i;
+            connected_idx[offset] =
+                static_cast<uint32_t>(element.connected_idx[j]);
+            connected_flux[offset] = element.connected_flux[j];
         }
-        
-        // Swap buffers
-        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
     }
+
+    deviceAllocate(device.material_idx, device.n_elems);
+    deviceAllocate(device.num_connections, device.n_elems);
+    deviceAllocate(device.connected_idx, connectivity_size);
+    deviceAllocate(device.connected_flux, connectivity_size);
+    deviceAllocate(device.materials, world.materials.size());
+    deviceAllocate(device.dynamic[0], device.n_elems);
+    deviceAllocate(device.dynamic[1], device.n_elems);
+
+    copyToDevice(device.material_idx, material_idx.data(), device.n_elems);
+    copyToDevice(device.num_connections, num_connections.data(), device.n_elems);
+    copyToDevice(device.connected_idx, connected_idx.data(), connectivity_size);
+    copyToDevice(device.connected_flux, connected_flux.data(), connectivity_size);
+    copyToDevice(device.materials, world.materials.data(), world.materials.size());
+    copyToDevice(device.dynamic[0], world.elements_dynamic.data(), device.n_elems);
+}
+
+template <int ConnectionsPerElement>
+__global__ void simulationIterationKernel(
+    const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+    const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+    const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+    const Material* __restrict__ materials,
+    const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ current,
+    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ next,
+    size_t n_elems) {
+    const size_t thread = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+
+    for (size_t i = thread; i < n_elems; i += stride) {
+        const ElementDynamic element = current[i];
+        const Material material = materials[material_idx[i]];
+        const int count = num_connections[i];
+        val_t total_flux = material.external_flow;
+
+        // Template specialization fully unrolls the small adjacency loop.  The
+        // conditional retains the exact operation count and order for boundary
+        // elements instead of padding them with arithmetic zeros.
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int j = 0; j < ConnectionsPerElement; ++j) {
+            if (j < count) {
+                const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(j) * n_elems + i;
+                const ElementDynamic neighbor = current[connected_idx[offset]];
+                total_flux +=
+                    (neighbor.current_energy - element.current_energy) *
+                    material.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[offset] * 0.25;
+            }
+        }
+
+        next[i].current_energy = element.current_energy + total_flux;
+        next[i].total_flux = element.total_flux + fabs(total_flux);
+    }
+}
+
+template <int ConnectionsPerElement>
+void launchSimulationIteration(DeviceMesh& device,
+                               const ElementDynamic* current,
+                               ElementDynamic* next,
+                               cudaStream_t stream) {
+    constexpr int threads_per_block = 256;
+    const size_t blocks_for_mesh =
+        (device.n_elems + threads_per_block - 1) / threads_per_block;
+    simulationIterationKernel<ConnectionsPerElement>
+        <<<static_cast<unsigned int>(blocks_for_mesh), threads_per_block, 0, stream>>>(
+            device.material_idx, device.num_connections, device.connected_idx,
+            device.connected_flux, device.materials, current, next, device.n_elems);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+}
+
+void launchSimulationIteration(DeviceMesh& device,
+                               const ElementDynamic* current,
+                               ElementDynamic* next,
+                               cudaStream_t stream) {
+    // Every supported degree gets a compile-time-unrolled CUDA specialization.
+    switch (device.connections_per_element) {
+        case 0: launchSimulationIteration<0>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 1: launchSimulationIteration<1>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 2: launchSimulationIteration<2>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 3: launchSimulationIteration<3>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 4: launchSimulationIteration<4>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 5: launchSimulationIteration<5>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 6: launchSimulationIteration<6>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 7: launchSimulationIteration<7>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 8: launchSimulationIteration<8>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        default:
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Unsupported CUDA mesh degree\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void emptySimulationKernel() {}
+
+// Capture all iterations once so the timed path needs only one CUDA graph
+// launch.  Consecutive graph nodes provide the required device-wide Jacobi
+// barrier without constraining the grid to cooperatively resident blocks.
+double runSimulation(DeviceMesh& device, const int n_iters) {
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t graph_instance = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeGlobal));
+
+    if (n_iters > 0) {
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+            const int read_buffer = iter & 1;
+            launchSimulationIteration(device, device.dynamic[read_buffer],
+                                      device.dynamic[read_buffer ^ 1], stream);
+        }
+    } else {
+        emptySimulationKernel<<<1, 1, 0, stream>>>();
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    }
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_instance, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    // Upload performs graph setup before the measured region without executing
+    // any node or changing the initial simulation state.
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(graph_instance, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_instance, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
+
+    float duration_ms = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&duration_ms, start, stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_instance));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));
+    return duration_ms;
+}
+
+void downloadSimulation(World& world, const DeviceMesh& device, int n_iters) {
+    const int final_buffer = n_iters > 0 && (n_iters & 1) ? 1 : 0;

+[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                              const char* file, int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n",
+                 file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+inline void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                      const char* file, int line) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        cudaFailure(error, expression, file, line);
+    }
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    cudaCheck((expression), #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__)
+
+// The mesh is transposed to a structure-of-arrays representation before it is
+// uploaded.  A warp therefore fetches consecutive material IDs, connection
+// counts, neighbor IDs, and coefficients instead of 32 widely separated AoS
+// records.  Device indices can be 32-bit because the host program's element
+// count is an int, cutting the dominant connectivity traffic in half.
+struct DeviceMesh {
+    uint8_t* material_idx = nullptr;
+    uint8_t* num_connections = nullptr;
+    uint32_t* connected_idx = nullptr;
+    val_t* connected_flux = nullptr;
+    Material* materials = nullptr;
+    ElementDynamic* dynamic[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    size_t n_elems = 0;
+    int connections_per_element = 0;
+
+    DeviceMesh() = default;
+    DeviceMesh(const DeviceMesh&) = delete;
+    DeviceMesh& operator=(const DeviceMesh&) = delete;
+
+    ~DeviceMesh() {
+        cudaFree(dynamic[1]);
+        cudaFree(dynamic[0]);
+        cudaFree(materials);
+        cudaFree(connected_flux);
+        cudaFree(connected_idx);
+        cudaFree(num_connections);
+        cudaFree(material_idx);
+    }
+};
+
+template <typename T>
+void deviceAllocate(T*& pointer, size_t count) {
+    static_assert(std::is_trivially_copyable_v<T>);
+    if (count != 0) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&pointer),
+                              count * sizeof(T)));
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void copyToDevice(T* destination, const T* source, size_t count) {
+    if (count != 0) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(destination, source, count * sizeof(T),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    }
+}
+
+void prepareDeviceMesh(const World& world, DeviceMesh& device) {
+    device.n_elems = world.elements_static.size();
+
+    if (device.n_elems > std::numeric_limits<uint32_t>::max()) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Mesh is too large for CUDA connectivity indices\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+    if (world.materials.size() > std::numeric_limits<uint8_t>::max()) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Too many material types for the CUDA mesh\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+
+    for (const ElementStatic& element : world.elements_static) {
+        if (element.num_connections > MAX_CONNECTIONS) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Element has more than %d connections\n",
+                         MAX_CONNECTIONS);
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        device.connections_per_element =
+            std::max(device.connections_per_element,
+                     static_cast<int>(element.num_connections));
+    }
+
+    std::vector<uint8_t> material_idx(device.n_elems);
+    std::vector<uint8_t> num_connections(device.n_elems);
+    const size_t connectivity_size =
+        device.n_elems * static_cast<size_t>(device.connections_per_element);
+    std::vector<uint32_t> connected_idx(connectivity_size);
+    std::vector<val_t> connected_flux(connectivity_size);
+
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < device.n_elems; ++i) {
+        const ElementStatic& element = world.elements_static[i];
+        if (element.material_idx >= world.materials.size()) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Element references an invalid material\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+
+        material_idx[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(element.material_idx);
+        num_connections[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(element.num_connections);
+        for (idx_t j = 0; j < element.num_connections; ++j) {
+            if (element.connected_idx[j] >= device.n_elems) {
+                std::fprintf(stderr, "Element references an invalid neighbor\n");
+                std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
             }
-            
-            // Update element state
-            ElementDynamic& elem_write = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i];
-            elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;
-            elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);
+            // Connection-major storage makes every load in a warp contiguous.
+            const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(j) * device.n_elems + i;
+            connected_idx[offset] =
+                static_cast<uint32_t>(element.connected_idx[j]);
+            connected_flux[offset] = element.connected_flux[j];
         }
-        
-        // Swap buffers
-        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
     }
+
+    deviceAllocate(device.material_idx, device.n_elems);
+    deviceAllocate(device.num_connections, device.n_elems);
+    deviceAllocate(device.connected_idx, connectivity_size);
+    deviceAllocate(device.connected_flux, connectivity_size);
+    deviceAllocate(device.materials, world.materials.size());
+    deviceAllocate(device.dynamic[0], device.n_elems);
+    deviceAllocate(device.dynamic[1], device.n_elems);
+
+    copyToDevice(device.material_idx, material_idx.data(), device.n_elems);
+    copyToDevice(device.num_connections, num_connections.data(), device.n_elems);
+    copyToDevice(device.connected_idx, connected_idx.data(), connectivity_size);
+    copyToDevice(device.connected_flux, connected_flux.data(), connectivity_size);
+    copyToDevice(device.materials, world.materials.data(), world.materials.size());
+    copyToDevice(device.dynamic[0], world.elements_dynamic.data(), device.n_elems);
+}
+
+template <int ConnectionsPerElement>
+__global__ void simulationIterationKernel(
+    const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+    const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+    const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+    const Material* __restrict__ materials,
+    const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ current,
+    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ next,
+    size_t n_elems) {
+    const size_t thread = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+
+    for (size_t i = thread; i < n_elems; i += stride) {
+        const ElementDynamic element = current[i];
+        const Material material = materials[material_idx[i]];
+        const int count = num_connections[i];
+        val_t total_flux = material.external_flow;
+
+        // Template specialization fully unrolls the small adjacency loop.  The
+        // conditional retains the exact operation count and order for boundary
+        // elements instead of padding them with arithmetic zeros.
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int j = 0; j < ConnectionsPerElement; ++j) {
+            if (j < count) {
+                const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(j) * n_elems + i;
+                const ElementDynamic neighbor = current[connected_idx[offset]];
+                total_flux +=
+                    (neighbor.current_energy - element.current_energy) *
+                    material.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[offset] * 0.25;
+            }
+        }
+
+        next[i].current_energy = element.current_energy + total_flux;
+        next[i].total_flux = element.total_flux + fabs(total_flux);
+    }
+}
+
+template <int ConnectionsPerElement>
+void launchSimulationIteration(DeviceMesh& device,
+                               const ElementDynamic* current,
+                               ElementDynamic* next,
+                               cudaStream_t stream) {
+    constexpr int threads_per_block = 256;
+    const size_t blocks_for_mesh =
+        (device.n_elems + threads_per_block - 1) / threads_per_block;
+    simulationIterationKernel<ConnectionsPerElement>
+        <<<static_cast<unsigned int>(blocks_for_mesh), threads_per_block, 0, stream>>>(
+            device.material_idx, device.num_connections, device.connected_idx,
+            device.connected_flux, device.materials, current, next, device.n_elems);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+}
+
+void launchSimulationIteration(DeviceMesh& device,
+                               const ElementDynamic* current,
+                               ElementDynamic* next,
+                               cudaStream_t stream) {
+    // Every supported degree gets a compile-time-unrolled CUDA specialization.
+    switch (device.connections_per_element) {
+        case 0: launchSimulationIteration<0>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 1: launchSimulationIteration<1>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 2: launchSimulationIteration<2>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 3: launchSimulationIteration<3>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 4: launchSimulationIteration<4>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 5: launchSimulationIteration<5>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 6: launchSimulationIteration<6>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 7: launchSimulationIteration<7>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 8: launchSimulationIteration<8>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        default:
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Unsupported CUDA mesh degree\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void emptySimulationKernel() {}
+
+// Capture all iterations once so the timed path needs only one CUDA graph
+// launch.  Consecutive graph nodes provide the required device-wide Jacobi
+// barrier without constraining the grid to cooperatively resident blocks.
+double runSimulation(DeviceMesh& device, const int n_iters) {
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t graph_instance = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeGlobal));
+
+    if (n_iters > 0) {
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+            const int read_buffer = iter & 1;
+            launchSimulationIteration(device, device.dynamic[read_buffer],
+                                      device.dynamic[read_buffer ^ 1], stream);
+        }
+    } else {
+        emptySimulationKernel<<<1, 1, 0, stream>>>();
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    }
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_instance, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    // Upload performs graph setup before the measured region without executing
+    // any node or changing the initial simulation state.
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(graph_instance, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_instance, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
+
+    float duration_ms = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&duration_ms, start, stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_instance));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));
+    return duration_ms;
+}
+
+void downloadSimulation(World& world, const DeviceMesh& device, int n_iters) {
+    const int final_buffer = n_iters > 0 && (n_iters & 1) ? 1 : 0;
+    if (device.n_elems != 0) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(),
+                              device.dynamic[final_buffer],
+                              device.n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
 }

+                 file, line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+inline void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                      const char* file, int line) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        cudaFailure(error, expression, file, line);
+    }
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    cudaCheck((expression), #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__)
+
+// The mesh is transposed to a structure-of-arrays representation before it is
+// uploaded.  A warp therefore fetches consecutive material IDs, connection
+// counts, neighbor IDs, and coefficients instead of 32 widely separated AoS
+// records.  Device indices can be 32-bit because the host program's element
+// count is an int, cutting the dominant connectivity traffic in half.
+struct DeviceMesh {
+    uint8_t* material_idx = nullptr;
+    uint8_t* num_connections = nullptr;
+    uint32_t* connected_idx = nullptr;
+    val_t* connected_flux = nullptr;
+    Material* materials = nullptr;
+    ElementDynamic* dynamic[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    size_t n_elems = 0;
+    int connections_per_element = 0;
+
+    DeviceMesh() = default;
+    DeviceMesh(const DeviceMesh&) = delete;
+    DeviceMesh& operator=(const DeviceMesh&) = delete;
+
+    ~DeviceMesh() {
+        cudaFree(dynamic[1]);
+        cudaFree(dynamic[0]);
+        cudaFree(materials);
+        cudaFree(connected_flux);
+        cudaFree(connected_idx);
+        cudaFree(num_connections);
+        cudaFree(material_idx);
+    }
+};
+
+template <typename T>
+void deviceAllocate(T*& pointer, size_t count) {
+    static_assert(std::is_trivially_copyable_v<T>);
+    if (count != 0) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&pointer),
+                              count * sizeof(T)));
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void copyToDevice(T* destination, const T* source, size_t count) {
+    if (count != 0) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(destination, source, count * sizeof(T),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    }
+}
+
+void prepareDeviceMesh(const World& world, DeviceMesh& device) {
+    device.n_elems = world.elements_static.size();
+
+    if (device.n_elems > std::numeric_limits<uint32_t>::max()) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Mesh is too large for CUDA connectivity indices\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+    if (world.materials.size() > std::numeric_limits<uint8_t>::max()) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Too many material types for the CUDA mesh\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+
+    for (const ElementStatic& element : world.elements_static) {
+        if (element.num_connections > MAX_CONNECTIONS) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Element has more than %d connections\n",
+                         MAX_CONNECTIONS);
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        device.connections_per_element =
+            std::max(device.connections_per_element,
+                     static_cast<int>(element.num_connections));
+    }
+
+    std::vector<uint8_t> material_idx(device.n_elems);
+    std::vector<uint8_t> num_connections(device.n_elems);
+    const size_t connectivity_size =
+        device.n_elems * static_cast<size_t>(device.connections_per_element);
+    std::vector<uint32_t> connected_idx(connectivity_size);
+    std::vector<val_t> connected_flux(connectivity_size);
+
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < device.n_elems; ++i) {
+        const ElementStatic& element = world.elements_static[i];
+        if (element.material_idx >= world.materials.size()) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Element references an invalid material\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+
+        material_idx[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(element.material_idx);
+        num_connections[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(element.num_connections);
+        for (idx_t j = 0; j < element.num_connections; ++j) {
+            if (element.connected_idx[j] >= device.n_elems) {
+                std::fprintf(stderr, "Element references an invalid neighbor\n");
+                std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
             }
-            
-            // Update element state
-            ElementDynamic& elem_write = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i];
-            elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;
-            elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);
+            // Connection-major storage makes every load in a warp contiguous.
+            const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(j) * device.n_elems + i;
+            connected_idx[offset] =
+                static_cast<uint32_t>(element.connected_idx[j]);
+            connected_flux[offset] = element.connected_flux[j];
         }
-        
-        // Swap buffers
-        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
     }
+
+    deviceAllocate(device.material_idx, device.n_elems);
+    deviceAllocate(device.num_connections, device.n_elems);
+    deviceAllocate(device.connected_idx, connectivity_size);
+    deviceAllocate(device.connected_flux, connectivity_size);
+    deviceAllocate(device.materials, world.materials.size());
+    deviceAllocate(device.dynamic[0], device.n_elems);
+    deviceAllocate(device.dynamic[1], device.n_elems);
+
+    copyToDevice(device.material_idx, material_idx.data(), device.n_elems);
+    copyToDevice(device.num_connections, num_connections.data(), device.n_elems);
+    copyToDevice(device.connected_idx, connected_idx.data(), connectivity_size);
+    copyToDevice(device.connected_flux, connected_flux.data(), connectivity_size);
+    copyToDevice(device.materials, world.materials.data(), world.materials.size());
+    copyToDevice(device.dynamic[0], world.elements_dynamic.data(), device.n_elems);
+}
+
+template <int ConnectionsPerElement>
+__global__ void simulationIterationKernel(
+    const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+    const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+    const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+    const Material* __restrict__ materials,
+    const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ current,
+    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ next,
+    size_t n_elems) {
+    const size_t thread = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+
+    for (size_t i = thread; i < n_elems; i += stride) {
+        const ElementDynamic element = current[i];
+        const Material material = materials[material_idx[i]];
+        const int count = num_connections[i];
+        val_t total_flux = material.external_flow;
+
+        // Template specialization fully unrolls the small adjacency loop.  The
+        // conditional retains the exact operation count and order for boundary
+        // elements instead of padding them with arithmetic zeros.
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int j = 0; j < ConnectionsPerElement; ++j) {
+            if (j < count) {
+                const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(j) * n_elems + i;
+                const ElementDynamic neighbor = current[connected_idx[offset]];
+                total_flux +=
+                    (neighbor.current_energy - element.current_energy) *
+                    material.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[offset] * 0.25;
+            }
+        }
+
+        next[i].current_energy = element.current_energy + total_flux;
+        next[i].total_flux = element.total_flux + fabs(total_flux);
+    }
+}
+
+template <int ConnectionsPerElement>
+void launchSimulationIteration(DeviceMesh& device,
+                               const ElementDynamic* current,
+                               ElementDynamic* next,
+                               cudaStream_t stream) {
+    constexpr int threads_per_block = 256;
+    const size_t blocks_for_mesh =
+        (device.n_elems + threads_per_block - 1) / threads_per_block;
+    simulationIterationKernel<ConnectionsPerElement>
+        <<<static_cast<unsigned int>(blocks_for_mesh), threads_per_block, 0, stream>>>(
+            device.material_idx, device.num_connections, device.connected_idx,
+            device.connected_flux, device.materials, current, next, device.n_elems);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+}
+
+void launchSimulationIteration(DeviceMesh& device,
+                               const ElementDynamic* current,
+                               ElementDynamic* next,
+                               cudaStream_t stream) {
+    // Every supported degree gets a compile-time-unrolled CUDA specialization.
+    switch (device.connections_per_element) {
+        case 0: launchSimulationIteration<0>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 1: launchSimulationIteration<1>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 2: launchSimulationIteration<2>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 3: launchSimulationIteration<3>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 4: launchSimulationIteration<4>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 5: launchSimulationIteration<5>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 6: launchSimulationIteration<6>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 7: launchSimulationIteration<7>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 8: launchSimulationIteration<8>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        default:
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Unsupported CUDA mesh degree\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void emptySimulationKernel() {}
+
+// Capture all iterations once so the timed path needs only one CUDA graph
+// launch.  Consecutive graph nodes provide the required device-wide Jacobi
+// barrier without constraining the grid to cooperatively resident blocks.
+double runSimulation(DeviceMesh& device, const int n_iters) {
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t graph_instance = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeGlobal));
+
+    if (n_iters > 0) {
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+            const int read_buffer = iter & 1;
+            launchSimulationIteration(device, device.dynamic[read_buffer],
+                                      device.dynamic[read_buffer ^ 1], stream);
+        }
+    } else {
+        emptySimulationKernel<<<1, 1, 0, stream>>>();
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    }
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_instance, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    // Upload performs graph setup before the measured region without executing
+    // any node or changing the initial simulation state.
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(graph_instance, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_instance, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
+
+    float duration_ms = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&duration_ms, start, stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_instance));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));
+    return duration_ms;
+}
+
+void downloadSimulation(World& world, const DeviceMesh& device, int n_iters) {
+    const int final_buffer = n_iters > 0 && (n_iters & 1) ? 1 : 0;
+    if (device.n_elems != 0) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(),
+                              device.dynamic[final_buffer],
+                              device.n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
 }

+template <int ConnectionsPerElement>
+__global__ void simulationIterationKernel(
+    const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
+    const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+    const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+    const Material* __restrict__ materials,
+    const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ current,
+    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ next,
+    size_t n_elems) {
+    const size_t thread = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+
+    for (size_t i = thread; i < n_elems; i += stride) {
+        const ElementDynamic element = current[i];
+        const Material material = materials[material_idx[i]];
+        const int count = num_connections[i];
+        val_t total_flux = material.external_flow;
+
+        // Template specialization fully unrolls the small adjacency loop.  The
+        // conditional retains the exact operation count and order for boundary
+        // elements instead of padding them with arithmetic zeros.
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int j = 0; j < ConnectionsPerElement; ++j) {
+            if (j < count) {
+                const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(j) * n_elems + i;
+                const ElementDynamic neighbor = current[connected_idx[offset]];
+                total_flux +=
+                    (neighbor.current_energy - element.current_energy) *
+                    material.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[offset] * 0.25;
+            }
+        }
+
+        next[i].current_energy = element.current_energy + total_flux;
+        next[i].total_flux = element.total_flux + fabs(total_flux);
+    }
+}
+
+template <int ConnectionsPerElement>
+void launchSimulationIteration(DeviceMesh& device,
+                               const ElementDynamic* current,
+                               ElementDynamic* next,
+                               cudaStream_t stream) {
+    constexpr int threads_per_block = 256;
+    const size_t blocks_for_mesh =
+        (device.n_elems + threads_per_block - 1) / threads_per_block;
+    simulationIterationKernel<ConnectionsPerElement>
+        <<<static_cast<unsigned int>(blocks_for_mesh), threads_per_block, 0, stream>>>(
+            device.material_idx, device.num_connections, device.connected_idx,
+            device.connected_flux, device.materials, current, next, device.n_elems);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+}
+
+void launchSimulationIteration(DeviceMesh& device,
+                               const ElementDynamic* current,
+                               ElementDynamic* next,
+                               cudaStream_t stream) {
+    // Every supported degree gets a compile-time-unrolled CUDA specialization.
+    switch (device.connections_per_element) {
+        case 0: launchSimulationIteration<0>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 1: launchSimulationIteration<1>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 2: launchSimulationIteration<2>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 3: launchSimulationIteration<3>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 4: launchSimulationIteration<4>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 5: launchSimulationIteration<5>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 6: launchSimulationIteration<6>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 7: launchSimulationIteration<7>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        case 8: launchSimulationIteration<8>(device, current, next, stream); break;
+        default:
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Unsupported CUDA mesh degree\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void emptySimulationKernel() {}
+
+// Capture all iterations once so the timed path needs only one CUDA graph
+// launch.  Consecutive graph nodes provide the required device-wide Jacobi
+// barrier without constraining the grid to cooperatively resident blocks.
+double runSimulation(DeviceMesh& device, const int n_iters) {
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t graph_instance = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeGlobal));
+
+    if (n_iters > 0) {
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+            const int read_buffer = iter & 1;
+            launchSimulationIteration(device, device.dynamic[read_buffer],
+                                      device.dynamic[read_buffer ^ 1], stream);
+        }
+    } else {
+        emptySimulationKernel<<<1, 1, 0, stream>>>();
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    }
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_instance, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    // Upload performs graph setup before the measured region without executing
+    // any node or changing the initial simulation state.
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(graph_instance, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
+
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_instance, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop, stream));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
+
+    float duration_ms = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&duration_ms, start, stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_instance));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(graph));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));
+    return duration_ms;
+}
+
+void downloadSimulation(World& world, const DeviceMesh& device, int n_iters) {
+    const int final_buffer = n_iters > 0 && (n_iters & 1) ? 1 : 0;
+    if (device.n_elems != 0) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(),
+                              device.dynamic[final_buffer],
+                              device.n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
 }

   130	
   131	#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
   132	    cudaCheck((expression), #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__)
   133	
   134	// The mesh is transposed to a structure-of-arrays representation before it is
   135	// uploaded.  A warp therefore fetches consecutive material IDs, connection
   136	// counts, neighbor IDs, and coefficients instead of 32 widely separated AoS
   137	// records.  Device indices can be 32-bit because the host program's element
   138	// count is an int, cutting the dominant connectivity traffic in half.
   139	struct DeviceMesh {
   140	    uint8_t* material_idx = nullptr;
   141	    uint8_t* num_connections = nullptr;
   142	    uint32_t* connected_idx = nullptr;
   143	    val_t* connected_flux = nullptr;
   144	    Material* materials = nullptr;
   145	    ElementDynamic* dynamic[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
   146	    size_t n_elems = 0;
   147	    int connections_per_element = 0;
   148	
   149	    DeviceMesh() = default;
   150	    DeviceMesh(const DeviceMesh&) = delete;
   151	    DeviceMesh& operator=(const DeviceMesh&) = delete;
   152	
   153	    ~DeviceMesh() {
   154	        cudaFree(dynamic[1]);
   155	        cudaFree(dynamic[0]);
   156	        cudaFree(materials);
   157	        cudaFree(connected_flux);
   158	        cudaFree(connected_idx);
   159	        cudaFree(num_connections);
   160	        cudaFree(material_idx);
   161	    }
   162	};
   163	
   164	template <typename T>
   165	void deviceAllocate(T*& pointer, size_t count) {
   166	    static_assert(std::is_trivially_copyable_v<T>);
   167	    if (count != 0) {
   168	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&pointer),
   169	                              count * sizeof(T)));
   170	    }
   171	}
   172	
   173	template <typename T>
   174	void copyToDevice(T* destination, const T* source, size_t count) {
   175	    if (count != 0) {
   176	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(destination, source, count * sizeof(T),
   177	                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   178	    }
   179	}
   180	
   181	void prepareDeviceMesh(const World& world, DeviceMesh& device) {
   182	    device.n_elems = world.elements_static.size();
   183	
   184	    if (device.n_elems > std::numeric_limits<uint32_t>::max()) {
   185	        std::fprintf(stderr, "Mesh is too large for CUDA connectivity indices\n");
   186	        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
   187	    }
   188	    if (world.materials.size() > std::numeric_limits<uint8_t>::max()) {
   189	        std::fprintf(stderr, "Too many material types for the CUDA mesh\n");
   190	        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
   191	    }
   192	
   193	    for (const ElementStatic& element : world.elements_static) {
   194	        if (element.num_connections > MAX_CONNECTIONS) {
   195	            std::fprintf(stderr, "Element has more than %d connections\n",
   196	                         MAX_CONNECTIONS);
   197	            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
   198	        }
   199	        device.connections_per_element =
   200	            std::max(device.connections_per_element,
   201	                     static_cast<int>(element.num_connections));
   202	    }
   203	
   204	    std::vector<uint8_t> material_idx(device.n_elems);
   205	    std::vector<uint8_t> num_connections(device.n_elems);
   206	    const size_t connectivity_size =
   207	        device.n_elems * static_cast<size_t>(device.connections_per_element);
   208	    std::vector<uint32_t> connected_idx(connectivity_size);
   209	    std::vector<val_t> connected_flux(connectivity_size);
   210	
   211	    for (size_t i = 0; i < device.n_elems; ++i) {
   212	        const ElementStatic& element = world.elements_static[i];
   213	        if (element.material_idx >= world.materials.size()) {
   214	            std::fprintf(stderr, "Element references an invalid material\n");
   215	            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
   216	        }
   217	
   218	        material_idx[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(element.material_idx);
   219	        num_connections[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(element.num_connections);
   220	        for (idx_t j = 0; j < element.num_connections; ++j) {
   221	            if (element.connected_idx[j] >= device.n_elems) {
   222	                std::fprintf(stderr, "Element references an invalid neighbor\n");
   223	                std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
   224	            }
   225	            // Connection-major storage makes every load in a warp contiguous.
   226	            const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(j) * device.n_elems + i;
   227	            connected_idx[offset] =
   228	                static_cast<uint32_t>(element.connected_idx[j]);
   229	            connected_flux[offset] = element.connected_flux[j];
   230	        }
   231	    }
   232	
   233	    deviceAllocate(device.material_idx, device.n_elems);
   234	    deviceAllocate(device.num_connections, device.n_elems);
   235	    deviceAllocate(device.connected_idx, connectivity_size);
   236	    deviceAllocate(device.connected_flux, connectivity_size);
   237	    deviceAllocate(device.materials, world.materials.size());
   238	    deviceAllocate(device.dynamic[0], device.n_elems);
   239	    deviceAllocate(device.dynamic[1], device.n_elems);
   240	
   241	    copyToDevice(device.material_idx, material_idx.data(), device.n_elems);
   242	    copyToDevice(device.num_connections, num_connections.data(), device.n_elems);
   243	    copyToDevice(device.connected_idx, connected_idx.data(), connectivity_size);
   244	    copyToDevice(device.connected_flux, connected_flux.data(), connectivity_size);
   245	    copyToDevice(device.materials, world.materials.data(), world.materials.size());
   246	    copyToDevice(device.dynamic[0], world.elements_dynamic.data(), device.n_elems);
   247	}
   248	
   249	template <int ConnectionsPerElement>
   250	__global__ void simulationIterationKernel(
   251	    const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_idx,
   252	    const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
   253	    const uint32_t* __restrict__ connected_idx,
   254	    const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
   255	    const Material* __restrict__ materials,
   256	    const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ current,
   257	    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ next,
   258	    size_t n_elems) {
   259	    const size_t thread = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
   260	    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
   261	
   262	    for (size_t i = thread; i < n_elems; i += stride) {
   263	        const ElementDynamic element = current[i];
   264	        const Material material = materials[material_idx[i]];
   265	        const int count = num_connections[i];
   266	        val_t total_flux = material.external_flow;
   267	
   268	        // Template specialization fully unrolls the small adjacency loop.  The
   269	        // conditional retains the exact operation count and order for boundary
   270	        // elements instead of padding them with arithmetic zeros.
   271	#pragma unroll
   272	        for (int j = 0; j < ConnectionsPerElement; ++j) {
   273	            if (j < count) {
   274	                const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(j) * n_elems + i;
   275	                const ElementDynamic neighbor = current[connected_idx[offset]];
   276	                total_flux +=
   277	                    (neighbor.current_energy - element.current_energy) *
   278	                    material.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[offset] * 0.25;
   279	            }
   280	        }
   281	
   282	        next[i].current_energy = element.current_energy + total_flux;
   283	        next[i].total_flux = element.total_flux + fabs(total_flux);
   284	    }
   285	}

   287	template <int ConnectionsPerElement>
   288	void launchSimulationIteration(DeviceMesh& device,
   289	                               const ElementDynamic* current,
   290	                               ElementDynamic* next,
   291	                               cudaStream_t stream) {
   292	    constexpr int threads_per_block = 256;
   293	    const size_t blocks_for_mesh =
   294	        (device.n_elems + threads_per_block - 1) / threads_per_block;
   295	    simulationIterationKernel<ConnectionsPerElement>
   296	        <<<static_cast<unsigned int>(blocks_for_mesh), threads_per_block, 0, stream>>>(
   297	            device.material_idx, device.num_connections, device.connected_idx,
   298	            device.connected_flux, device.materials, current, next, device.n_elems);
   299	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   300	}

   302	void launchSimulationIteration(DeviceMesh& device,
   303	                               const ElementDynamic* current,
   304	                               ElementDynamic* next,
   305	                               cudaStream_t stream) {
   306	    // Every supported degree gets a compile-time-unrolled CUDA specialization.
   307	    switch (device.connections_per_element) {
   308	        case 0: launchSimulationIteration<0>(device, current, next, stream); break;
   309	        case 1: launchSimulationIteration<1>(device, current, next, stream); break;
   310	        case 2: launchSimulationIteration<2>(device, current, next, stream); break;
   311	        case 3: launchSimulationIteration<3>(device, current, next, stream); break;
   312	        case 4: launchSimulationIteration<4>(device, current, next, stream); break;
   313	        case 5: launchSimulationIteration<5>(device, current, next, stream); break;
   314	        case 6: launchSimulationIteration<6>(device, current, next, stream); break;
   315	        case 7: launchSimulationIteration<7>(device, current, next, stream); break;
   316	        case 8: launchSimulationIteration<8>(device, current, next, stream); break;
   317	        default:
   318	            std::fprintf(stderr, "Unsupported CUDA mesh degree\n");
   319	            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
   320	    }
   321	}

   323	__global__ void emptySimulationKernel() {}
   324	
   325	// Capture all iterations once so the timed path needs only one CUDA graph
   326	// launch.  Consecutive graph nodes provide the required device-wide Jacobi
   327	// barrier without constraining the grid to cooperatively resident blocks.
   328	double runSimulation(DeviceMesh& device, const int n_iters) {
   329	    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
   330	    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
   331	    cudaGraphExec_t graph_instance = nullptr;
   332	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
   333	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeGlobal));
   334	
   335	    if (n_iters > 0) {
   336	        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
   337	            const int read_buffer = iter & 1;
   338	            launchSimulationIteration(device, device.dynamic[read_buffer],
   339	                                      device.dynamic[read_buffer ^ 1], stream);
   340	        }
   341	    } else {
   342	        emptySimulationKernel<<<1, 1, 0, stream>>>();
   343	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   344	    }
   345	
   346	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph));
   347	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph_instance, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
   348	    // Upload performs graph setup before the measured region without executing
   349	    // any node or changing the initial simulation state.
   350	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphUpload(graph_instance, stream));
   351	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
   352	
   353	    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
   354	    cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
   355	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
   356	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
   357	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start, stream));
   358	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(graph_instance, stream));
   359	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop, stream));
   360	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
   361	
   362	    float duration_ms = 0.0F;
   363	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&duration_ms, start, stop));
   364	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stop));
   365	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
   366	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphExecDestroy(graph_instance));
   367	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(graph));
   368	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));
   369	    return duration_ms;
   370	}
